#### COMBAT TACTICS ###########################################################
#
# NOTE: tactics must start with "tactic_" in their name to work with techs.
###############################################################################

phases = { # phase = no is the standard phase
	static
	cqc
	manuever
	breakthrough
	withdrawal
	op_destruction
	urban_warfare
	seize_bridge
	hold_bridge
	amphibious_assault
}

######### PHASE 0 #########

tactic_Preliminary_Bombardment = {
	is_attacker = yes
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = yes
		phase = no
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #SIMPLE TACTIC TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_simple_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #RISK AVERSION TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_risk_aversion_desc 
			skill < 21
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { 
		tooltip =  tt_artillery_battalion_in_combat
			OR = {#ARTILLERY TACTIC
				has_unit_type = pack_artillery_horse_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = artillery_horse_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = artillery_mot_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = heavy_artillery_mot_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = rocket_artillery_horse_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = rocket_artillery_mot_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = light_self_propelled_gun_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = medium_self_propelled_gun_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = heavy_self_propelled_gun_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = modern_self_propelled_gun_battalion_line
				has_unit_type = armoured_rocket_battalion_line
			}
		}
		NOT = { is_fighting_in_terrain = urban }
	}
	active = no
	base = {
		factor = 6
		#RECON FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.4
		}
		#SIMPLE FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill < 4
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill < 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill < 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage < 1
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage < 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage < 3
		}
		modifier = {# SIMPLE_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = fast_planner
		}
		modifier = {# SIMPLE_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = new_blood
		}				modifier = {# SIMPLE_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = inflexible_strategist 
		}
		modifier = {# TENACIOUS DEFENSE / ACADEMY SCHOLARSHIPS
			add = 4
			has_trait = inflexible_strategist 
			owner = {
				OR = {
				has_idea = tenacious_defense_spirit
				has_idea = academy_scholarships_spirit
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {# ARTILLERY_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = bearer_of_artillery
		}
		modifier = {# smoke and fire
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = smoke_and_fire_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# ARTILLERY_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = fortress_buster
		}
		modifier = {# ARTILLERY_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = artillery_expert
		}
		modifier = {# ARTILLERY_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = artillery_officer
		}
		modifier = {# ENGINEERING AND ARTILLERY SCHOOLS
			add = 4
			has_trait = artillery_officer
			owner = {
				has_idea = superior_firepower_academy_spirit
			}
		}		
		modifier = {# OFFENSIVE_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = offensive_doctrine
		}
		modifier = {# OFFENSIVE_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = aggressive_assaulter
		}
		modifier = { # WW1_TACTICS
			add = 4
			has_trait = old_guard
		}
		modifier = {# static warfare spirit
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = static_warfare_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# GREAT WAR LEGACY
			add = 4
			has_trait = old_guard
			owner = {
				has_idea = great_war_legacy_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = { #RISK AVERSION
			add = 2
			has_trait = trait_cautious
		}
		modifier = { # TACTIC IS NOT RISKY
			factor = 2
			skill > 0
		}

	}
	picture = positional_default_atk
	countered_by = tactic_Counter_Barrage

	attacker = -0.25
	defender = -0.35
	combat_width = -0.33
	attacker_movement_speed = -0.75
	defender_org_damage_modifier = 0.5
}

tactic_Reconnaissance_in_force = {
	is_attacker = yes
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = yes
		phase = no
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #ELABORATE TACTIC TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_elaborate_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #RISK TOLERANCE TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_risk_tolerance_desc 
			skill < 21
		}
		#ASYMETRIC TRIGGER
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_asymmetric_desc
			OR = {
					recon_advantage > 0.4
					skill_advantage > 1
					skill > 2
			}
			OR = {
					has_trait = flexible_tactician
					has_trait = trickster
					has_trait = commando
					has_trait = camouflage_expert
					has_trait = expert_improviser
					has_trait = guerilla_fighter
					has_trait = ambusher
					has_trait = adaptable
					skill > 1
			}
		}
		#ADVANTAGE RECON TRIGGER
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_recon_trigger_desc
			OR = {
				skill_advantage > 1
				recon_advantage > 0.2
				is_winning = yes
			}
		}
		NOT = { is_fighting_in_terrain = urban }
	}
	active = no
	base = { 
		factor = 4
		#RECON FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.4
		}
 		#COMPLICATED FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 4
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 1
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 3
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = thorough_planner
		}
		modifier = {# flexible organization
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = flexible_organization_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = skilled_staffer
		}
		modifier = {# BEST OF THE BEST
			add = 2
			has_trait = skilled_staffer
			owner = {
				has_idea = best_of_the_best_spirit
			}
		}

		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = brilliant_strategist
		}
		modifier = {# BOLD ATTACK / BEST OF THE BEST
			add = 4
			has_trait = brilliant_strategist
			owner = {
				OR = {
				has_idea = bold_attack_spirit
				has_idea = best_of_the_best_spirit
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = expert_delegator
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = organizer
		}
		modifier = {# METICULOUS PREPARATION
			add = 4
			has_trait = organizer
			owner = {
				has_idea = meticulous_preperation_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# INFANTRY_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = infantry_leader
		}
		modifier = {# INFANTRY_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = infantry_expert
		}
		modifier = {# INFANTRY_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = skirmisher
		}
		modifier = {# aggessive reconnaissance
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = aggressive_reconnaisance_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# INFANTRY_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = infantry_officer
		}
		modifier = {# QUEEN OF BATTLE
			add = 4
			has_trait = infantry_officer
			owner = {
				has_idea = mass_assault_academy_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# OFFENSIVE_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = offensive_doctrine
		}
		modifier = {# OFFENSIVE_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = aggressive_assaulter
		}		modifier = {# MANUEVER_SLIGHT
			add = 2

		}
		modifier = {# MANUEVER_SLIGHT

		}
		modifier = {# TRICKY_TACTICS
			add = 2

		}
		modifier = {# THEATRE TRAINING
			add = 2
			has_trait = flexible_tactician
			owner = {
				has_idea = theatre_training_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# TRICKY_TACTICS

		}
		modifier = {# INVENTIVE LEADERSHIP
			add = 2

				has_idea = inventive_leadership_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# guerilla warfare
			factor = 6
			owner = {
				has_idea = guerilla_warfare_corps_spirit
			}

		}

			add = 2
			has_trait = commando
		}
		modifier = {# INVENTIVE LEADERSHIP
			add = 2
			has_trait = commando
			owner = {
				has_idea = inventive_leadership_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# TRICKY_TACTICS	
			add = 2
			has_trait = camouflage_expert
		}
		modifier = {# TRICKY_TACTICS
			add = 2
			has_trait = expert_improviser
		}
		modifier = {# TRICKY_TACTICS
			add = 2
			has_trait = guerilla_fighter
		}
		modifier = {# TRICKY_TACTICS
			add = 2
			has_trait = ambusher
		}
		modifier = { # RISK TOLERANCE
			add = 2
			has_trait = trait_reckless
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 1
			skill_advantage > 1
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 2
			skill_advantage > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 3
			skill_advantage > 3
		}
		modifier = {# operational reserves
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = operational_reserve_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# strategic reserves
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = strategic_reserve_spirit
			}
		}		
	}
	picture = positional_default_atk

	attacker = -0.15

	attacker_movement_speed = 0.5

	defender_org_damage_modifier = 0.10
	phase = manuever 
}

tactic_Urban_Reconnaissance = {
	is_attacker = yes
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = yes
		phase = no
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #SIMPLE TACTIC TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_simple_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		is_fighting_in_terrain = urban
	}
	active = yes
	base = {
		factor = 1000
		modifier = {# operational reserves
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = operational_reserve_spirit
			}
		}
	}
	picture = positional_default_atk
	countered_by = tactic_Urban_Concealment

	attacker = -0.25
	defender = -0.30
	attacker_movement_speed = 0.5
	attacker_org_damage_modifier = -0.4
	defender_org_damage_modifier = -0.4
	phase = urban_warfare
}

tactic_Assault = {
	is_attacker = yes
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = yes
		phase = no
		NOT = { is_fighting_in_terrain = urban }
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #SIMPLE TACTIC TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_simple_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_offensive_inf_desc
			OR = {
				hardness < 0.5
				AND = {		
					OR = {	
						hardness > 0.5	
						has_unit_type = light_cavalry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_cavalry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = light_camelry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_camelry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_light_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_heavy_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = militia_light_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = militia_heavy_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = penal_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_light_bicycle_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_heavy_bicycle_battalion_line 
						has_unit_type = infantry_light_motorized_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_heavy_motorized_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_heavy_mechanized_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = marine_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = mountaineer_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = paratrooper_horse_battalion_line
 						has_unit_type = amphibious_mechanized_battalion_line 	
					}		
					OR = {
						hardness < 0.19
						has_unit_type = armoured_car_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = armoured_car_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = scout_car_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = light_infantry_support_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = medium_infantry_support_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_infantry_support_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = modern_infantry_support_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = light_assault_gun_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = medium_assault_gun_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_assault_gun_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = modern_assault_gun_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = super_heavy_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = ger_landkruiser_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = light_support_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = medium_support_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_support_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = light_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = medium_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = modern_armor_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_heavy_mechanized_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = amphibious_mechanized_battalion_line 
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	active = yes
	base = {
		factor = 6
		#RECON FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.4
		}
		#INFANTRY FACTOR	
		modifier = {
		factor = 2
		OR = {
			has_unit_type = light_cavalry_horse_battalion_line
			has_unit_type = heavy_cavalry_horse_battalion_line
			has_unit_type = light_camelry_horse_battalion_line
			has_unit_type = heavy_camelry_horse_battalion_line
			has_unit_type = infantry_light_horse_battalion_line
			has_unit_type = infantry_heavy_horse_battalion_line

tactic_CQC_Clearing_Operations = {
	is_attacker = yes
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = yes
		phase = cqc
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #ELABORATE TACTIC TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_elaborate_desc

		}

		tooltip = t_risk_aversion_desc 
			skill < 21
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_offensive_inf_desc
			OR = {
				hardness < 0.5
				AND = {		
					OR = {	
						hardness > 0.5	
						has_unit_type = light_cavalry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_cavalry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = light_camelry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = heavy_camelry_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_light_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_heavy_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = militia_light_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = militia_heavy_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = penal_horse_battalion_line
						has_unit_type = infantry_light_bicycle_battalion_line

tactic_Ambush = {
	is_attacker = no
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = no
		phase = manuever
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { 
		tooltip = t_complicated_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #RISK TOLERANCE TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_risk_tolerance_desc 
			skill < 21
		}
		skill > 1
		#ASYMETRIC TRIGGER
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_asymmetric_desc
			OR = {
					recon_advantage > 0.4
					skill_advantage > 1
					skill > 2
			}
			OR = {
					has_trait = flexible_tactician
					has_trait = trickster
					has_trait = commando
					has_trait = camouflage_expert
					has_trait = expert_improviser

					has_trait = adaptable
					skill > 1
			}
		}
		#ADVANTAGE RECON TRIGGER
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_recon_trigger_desc
			OR = {
				skill_advantage > 1
				recon_advantage > 0.2
				is_winning = yes
			}
		}
	}
	active = no
	base = {
		factor = 2
		#RECON FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.4

		tooltip = t_elaborate_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #RISK TOLERANCE TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_risk_tolerance_desc 
			skill < 21
		}

		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_asymmetric_desc
			OR = {
					recon_advantage > 0.4
					skill_advantage > 1
					skill > 2
			}
			OR = {
					has_trait = flexible_tactician

tactic_Strategic_Withdrawl = {
	is_attacker = no
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = no
		phase = withdrawal
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #ELABORATE TACTIC TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_elaborate_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #RISK AVERSION TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_risk_aversion_desc 
			skill < 21
		}
	}
	active = no
	base = {
		factor = 4
		#RECON FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.4
		}
 		#COMPLICATED FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 4
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 1
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 3
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = thorough_planner
		}
		modifier = {# flexible organization
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = flexible_organization_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = skilled_staffer
		}
		modifier = {# BEST OF THE BEST
			add = 2
			has_trait = skilled_staffer
			owner = {
				has_idea = best_of_the_best_spirit
			}
		}

		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = brilliant_strategist
		}
		modifier = {# BOLD ATTACK / BEST OF THE BEST
			add = 4
			has_trait = brilliant_strategist
			owner = {
				OR = {
				has_idea = bold_attack_spirit
				has_idea = best_of_the_best_spirit
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = expert_delegator
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = organizer
		}
		modifier = {# METICULOUS PREPARATION
			add = 4
			has_trait = organizer
			owner = {
				has_idea = meticulous_preperation_spirit
			}
		}
	
		modifier = {# DEFENSIVE_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = defensive_doctrine
		}
		modifier = {# DEFENSIVE_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = unyielding_defender
		}
		modifier = { #RISK AVERSION
			add = 2
			has_trait = trait_cautious
		}
		modifier = { # TACTIC IS NOT RISKY
			factor = 2
			skill > 0
		}
	}
	picture = strategic_withdrawl_def

	attacker = -0.25
	defender = -0.1
	attacker_movement_speed = -0.5
	attacker_org_damage_modifier = 0.25
	defender_org_damage_modifier = -0.1
	phase = manuever

tactic_Operational_Destruction = {
	is_attacker = yes
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = yes
		phase = op_destruction
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { 
		tooltip = t_complicated_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #RISK TOLERANCE TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_risk_tolerance_desc 
			skill < 21
		}
		skill > 1
	}
	active = no
	base = {
		factor = 2
		#RECON FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			recon_advantage > 0.4
		}
 		#COMPLICATED FACTOR
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill > 4
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 1
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			skill_advantage > 3
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = thorough_planner
		}
		modifier = {# flexible organization
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				has_idea = flexible_organization_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = skilled_staffer
		}
		modifier = {# BEST OF THE BEST
			add = 2
			has_trait = skilled_staffer
			owner = {
				has_idea = best_of_the_best_spirit
			}
		}

		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = brilliant_strategist
		}
		modifier = {# BOLD ATTACK / BEST OF THE BEST
			add = 4
			has_trait = brilliant_strategist
			owner = {
				OR = {
				has_idea = bold_attack_spirit
				has_idea = best_of_the_best_spirit
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = expert_delegator
		}
		modifier = {# COMPLICATED_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = organizer
		}
		modifier = {# METICULOUS PREPARATION
			add = 4
			has_trait = organizer
			owner = {
				has_idea = meticulous_preperation_spirit
			}
		}
		modifier = {# OFFENSIVE_SLIGHT
			add = 2
			has_trait = offensive_doctrine
		}
		modifier = {# OFFENSIVE_HIGHER
			add = 4
			has_trait = aggressive_assaulter
		}	
		modifier = { # RISK TOLERANCE
			add = 2
			has_trait = trait_reckless
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 1
			skill_advantage > 1
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 2
			skill_advantage > 2
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 3
			skill_advantage > 3
		}				
	}
	picture = operational_destruction_atk

	attacker = 0.3
	defender = -0.3
	attacker_movement_speed = 0.2
	attacker_org_damage_modifier = -0.25
	defender_org_damage_modifier = -0.75
}

tactic_Evasion_Op = {
	is_attacker = no
	trigger = {
		is_attacker = no
		phase = op_destruction
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #SIMPLE TACTIC TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_simple_desc
			skill < 20
		}
		custom_trigger_tooltip = { #RISK AVERSION TOOLTIP
		tooltip = t_risk_aversion_desc 
			skill < 21
		}
		#ASYMETRIC TRIGGER
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_asymmetric_desc
			OR = {
					recon_advantage > 0.4
					skill_advantage > 1
					skill > 2
			}
			OR = {
					has_trait = flexible_tactician
					has_trait = trickster
					has_trait = commando
					has_trait = camouflage_expert
					has_trait = expert_improviser
					has_trait = guerilla_fighter
					has_trait = ambusher
					has_trait = adaptable
					skill > 1
			}
		}
		#ADVANTAGE RECON TRIGGER
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = t_recon_trigger_desc

				skill_advantage > 1
